    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1

            try:

                pass

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:
>           raise OSError(

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:
>           raise OSError(

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:
>           raise OSError(

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:
>           raise OSError(
                "could not create numbered dir with prefix "

            )

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:
>           raise OSError(

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:
>           raise OSError(

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:
>           raise OSError(

        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""

    def make_numbered_dir(root: Path, prefix: str, mode: int = 0o700) -> Path:
        """Create a directory with an increased number as suffix for the given prefix."""
        for i in range(10):
            # try up to 10 times to create the directory
            max_existing = max(map(parse_num, find_suffixes(root, prefix)), default=-1)
            new_number = max_existing + 1
            new_path = root.joinpath(f"{prefix}{new_number}")
            try:
                new_path.mkdir(mode=mode)
            except Exception:
                pass
            else:
                _force_symlink(root, prefix + "current", new_path)
                return new_path
        else:
